### Identity (reviewed)

Original 2016 platform/action game source by Juan J. Martinez for the ZX Spectrum 48K, with room data, enemies, collectibles, keyboard/joystick selection and a bespoke loader. This is the native game, not an emulator wrapper.
- https://github.com/reidrac/castaway-zx/blob/d4746cf4f70d4ffe511885eca5b195a4b0264c95/main.c
- https://github.com/reidrac/castaway-zx/blob/d4746cf4f70d4ffe511885eca5b195a4b0264c95/Makefile
- https://github.com/reidrac/castaway-zx/blob/d4746cf4f70d4ffe511885eca5b195a4b0264c95/gpl-3.0.txt
- https://github.com/reidrac/castaway-zx/blob/d4746cf4f70d4ffe511885eca5b195a4b0264c95/README.md
- https://github.com/reidrac/castaway-zx/commit/d4746cf4f70d4ffe511885eca5b195a4b0264c95

### Classification (reviewed)

2971-line main.c contains sprite setup, room persistence, intro/menu, enemy/player gameplay and scoring; generated asset pipeline and original PNG/map/song inputs are present. Historical code is explicitly unsupported and dependencies are dated. GPL-3.0-or-later code header and GPL text; README licenses game assets CC-BY-SA 2.0 and notes separate third-party tool/library terms.

### Identity (reviewed)

Simon Springett’s 1984 ZX Spectrum BASIC shepherding game preserved as readable source with tape/audio artifacts and a source-to-tape utility. Shepherd, sheep chain and pursuing wolf are simulated by the game itself.
- https://github.com/paulspringett/good-shepherd/blob/dc8d1d365836f2a60298a826f7a4e913e50b07b6/GoodShepherd.bas
- https://github.com/paulspringett/good-shepherd/blob/dc8d1d365836f2a60298a826f7a4e913e50b07b6/README.md
- https://github.com/paulspringett/good-shepherd/blob/dc8d1d365836f2a60298a826f7a4e913e50b07b6/bas2tap/bas2tap.c
- https://github.com/paulspringett/good-shepherd/commit/dc8d1d365836f2a60298a826f7a4e913e50b07b6

### Classification (reviewed)

316-line BASIC listing implements grass placement/eating, sheep-following history, wolf pursuit, time/feed/score limits, collision losses and level changes. TAP/TZX/WAV outputs are included. README expressly licenses Good Shepherd CC-BY-NC-SA 3.0; bundled bas2tap has separate M. van der Heide notices. Noncommercial restriction means do not describe whole repository as unrestricted open source.
- https://github.com/paulspringett/good-shepherd/blob/dc8d1d365836f2a60298a826f7a4e913e50b07b6/GoodShepherd.bas

Text adventure independently implemented in portable C and then hand-ported to Z80 for CP/M and the 48K Spectrum. A turn-limited lighthouse crisis supports inventory/object puzzles and two solutions.

### Identity (reviewed)

Mouse-aimed native Spectrum Next arcade defense game. Players eliminate approaching Jeffs using delayed explosions, charge management and numerous bonuses while advancing through areas.
- https://github.com/ptsochantaris/operation-jeff/blob/044e771b21accc175835206e6b939155accdff04/src/game.c

Substantial multi-file implementation: gameLoop updates bombs, enemies, bonuses and stats; jeff.c provides enemy state machines and collision handling. Sprite/tilemap/Layer2/copper/DMA code and original resources are present. README still calls it work in progress. No root or nested license file found in reviewed tree, and README gives no overall redistribution grant; describe as source-available with license unspecified.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original Spectrum action/puzzle game built around integer-optimized flocking/Boids behavior. The player manipulates levels to clear a swarm under a time limit.
- https://github.com/derekfountain/the-virus/blob/c80b79bb471a4deb15365ac25beeafdc4b3c107e/main.c
- https://github.com/derekfountain/the-virus/blob/c80b79bb471a4deb15365ac25beeafdc4b3c107e/swarm.c
- https://github.com/derekfountain/the-virus/blob/c80b79bb471a4deb15365ac25beeafdc4b3c107e/virion.c
- https://github.com/derekfountain/the-virus/blob/c80b79bb471a4deb15365ac25beeafdc4b3c107e/makefile
- https://github.com/derekfountain/the-virus/blob/c80b79bb471a4deb15365ac25beeafdc4b3c107e/license.txt
- https://github.com/derekfountain/the-virus/blob/c80b79bb471a4deb15365ac25beeafdc4b3c107e/README.md
- https://github.com/derekfountain/the-virus/commit/c80b79bb471a4deb15365ac25beeafdc4b3c107e

### Classification (reviewed)

Game loop initializes levels/player/swarm, updates moving cells and virions, and handles time-up/completion. swarm.c explicitly distinguishes moving-cell levels and optimizes alternating updates. Twenty level modules/data, audio and TAP/RZX artifacts are present. GPL version 2 text in license.txt, with GPL-2.0-or-later source/makefile headers. Third-party font and Boids contributions credited in README.
- https://github.com/derekfountain/the-virus/blob/c80b79bb471a4deb15365ac25beeafdc4b3c107e/main.c

### Identity (reviewed)

Native ecological hunting/puzzle game in which grass spreads, grazers eat/reproduce/starve and a hunter intervenes in an evolving world. One source tree supports Spectrum, C64, MSX and Master System.
- https://github.com/snauts/grazers/blob/fe0567e1a942e79dd991ea19863780de5d7a30c1/main.c
- https://github.com/snauts/grazers/blob/fe0567e1a942e79dd991ea19863780de5d7a30c1/Makefile

2746-line main.c has per-machine startup/hardware code, hunter movement, ecosystem queues, environmental disasters, level endings and title/game loops. manual.tex explains simulation rules and objectives. ### Identity (reviewed)

Alfonso Saavedra’s Metroid-inspired Spectrum fan game using La Churrera. The player explores a spaceship and carries fourteen eggs one at a time to an incubator before reaching the control room.
- https://github.com/son-link/SamiTroid/blob/9155ca87594a47ac200dbf758314d184f0edf0ee/dev/mainloop.h
- https://github.com/son-link/SamiTroid/blob/9155ca87594a47ac200dbf758314d184f0edf0ee/dev/config.h

- https://github.com/son-link/SamiTroid/commit/9155ca87594a47ac200dbf758314d184f0edf0ee

### Classification (reviewed)

889-line main loop and engine are accompanied by game-specific map/config/art/music and sami.spt scripting that increments delivered-egg state and unlocks exits. Substantial native game content, not just an unconfigured engine template. README licenses the game CC-BY-NC-SA 3.0 and credits La Churrera/Mojon Twins, graphics and music contributors. Metroid trademark attribution does not establish Nintendo asset rights.
- https://github.com/son-link/SamiTroid/blob/9155ca87594a47ac200dbf758314d184f0edf0ee/dev/mainloop.h

### Identity (reviewed)

Wordle-style five-letter word puzzle implemented for the ZX80, ZX81 and Spectrum/Timex family. Six guesses receive positional/letter feedback and feed a running score.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Worms-like turn-based artillery game for CPC 464/6128 with destructible procedural terrain, teams, hotseat/CPU opponents, multiple weapons and persistent score storage. Includes attract-demo programs.
- https://github.com/vaspervnp/CreepersCPC/blob/d126deb1c2d8750b4e63b882ef7262d5b0990520/src/game.asm
- https://github.com/vaspervnp/CreepersCPC/blob/d126deb1c2d8750b4e63b882ef7262d5b0990520/src/weapons.asm
- https://github.com/vaspervnp/CreepersCPC/blob/d126deb1c2d8750b4e63b882ef7262d5b0990520/src/terrain.asm
- https://github.com/vaspervnp/CreepersCPC/blob/d126deb1c2d8750b4e63b882ef7262d5b0990520/Makefile
- https://github.com/vaspervnp/CreepersCPC/blob/d126deb1c2d8750b4e63b882ef7262d5b0990520/README.md

- https://github.com/vaspervnp/CreepersCPC/tree/d126deb1c2d8750b4e63b882ef7262d5b0990520

### Classification (reviewed)

game.asm implements turn/resolve/pause/game-over state and an AI aiming plan; weapons.asm implements charge/release, hitscan/projectiles, ammo, collision and ballistic integration; terrain modules and raw FDC score code are present. ### Identity (reviewed)

Raylib-based Windows/Linux/Web restoration port of Richard Phipps’s 2008 Chaos Groove, itself a remake of Julian Gollop’s Spectrum Chaos: Battle of the Wizards. Preserves native board/spell gameplay rather than wrapping a Spectrum CPU emulator.
- https://github.com/h8man/ChaosGroove/blob/df41128d60818af1b419ad0160d35efbc54e1b6f/board.cpp
- https://github.com/h8man/ChaosGroove/blob/df41128d60818af1b419ad0160d35efbc54e1b6f/spell.cpp
- https://github.com/h8man/ChaosGroove/blob/df41128d60818af1b419ad0160d35efbc54e1b6f/bridge.cpp
- https://github.com/h8man/ChaosGroove/blob/df41128d60818af1b419ad0160d35efbc54e1b6f/makefile

- https://github.com/h8man/ChaosGroove/blob/df41128d60818af1b419ad0160d35efbc54e1b6f/README.md
- https://github.com/h8man/ChaosGroove/commit/df41128d60818af1b419ad0160d35efbc54e1b6f

### Classification (reviewed)

board.cpp and spell.cpp each exceed 1000 lines, implementing ownership, engagement rules, creature creation, spell targeting/casting and board effects. bridge.cpp adapts graphics/input to raylib. Netplay restoration remains planned; no execution or gameplay-fidelity verification. Custom noncommercial source license permits use/modification but forbids selling game or modifications. Third-party raylib/asset/library terms remain separate. CREDITS’ original-Chaos distribution statement does not replace the remake/port license.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reservoir Gods' original Atari ST vegetable-juggling game source, identified by the HOTPOT.PRJ header as Double Juggle Vegatabobble: This Is Vegetable Juggling (2002). The repository currently contains the Hotpot implementation rather than a separate new game per directory.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original MIDI rhythm game for Atari ST: one or two players guide dragons to collect notes while the ST sound chip plays selected MIDI tracks. Actual gameplay, MIDI parsing and native GEM graphics are present.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Atari GEM adaptation of John Elliott’s C/GEM 2048, retaining his sliding-block board logic and GEM front end while adding an m68k Atari build and NatFeats support.
- https://github.com/mfro0/2048/blob/9822d9f1ad2b54b1024b6bd81022d5c0a45a353d/sources/2048game.c

### Classification (reviewed)

Reviewed board initialization/merge implementation and multilib target rules. Included 2048.txt describes the older PC GEM origin, so that text alone is not proof of Atari execution. GPL-2.0-or-later in implementation headers; COPYING contains GPLv2.

### Identity (reviewed)

Ataxx-style RISC OS desktop board game in RiscLua, including full board/rules classes and native Toolbox wrapper integration. Source and a bundled standalone runtime application are present.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native C/OSLib recreation of Lode Runner for RISC OS, with destructible terrain, climbing/digging, enemy navigation, sprite rendering, WIMP integration, configurable scaling and saved level progress.
- https://github.com/Niisoks/RiscOS-Lode_Runner/blob/e7e411207711a6689cc52ae766bc087cee71aa71/!LodeRunner/c/main

### Identity (reviewed)

Bare-metal X68000 wireframe battle-racing game with local one/two-player modes, three tracks, drift/boost/slipstream mechanics, fixed-step simulation and double-buffered display. Boots from XDF without Human68k runtime.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Game Boy Advance Pump It Up-style rhythm simulator consuming StepMania SSC charts. Judge.cpp implements timing windows, hold judging and score feedback; SongScene orchestrates native sprites, audio, chart play and multiplayer/hardware effects. It does not emulate an arcade CPU.
- https://github.com/afska/piugba/blob/ecc5f5226eb76f06ac91e89b96a6c378322f4e45/README.md
- https://github.com/afska/piugba/blob/ecc5f5226eb76f06ac91e89b96a6c378322f4e45/LICENSE

- https://github.com/afska/piugba/commit/ecc5f5226eb76f06ac91e89b96a6c378322f4e45

### Classification (reviewed)

Deep gameplay implementation and detailed build pipeline; chart import/content packaging is required for an actual song-filled ROM. Flashcart-specific high-quality media features are documented, not tested. MIT project licence. Bundled libraries carry their own notices under .licenses; charts/audio/video supplied by users have separate rights, and clean builds contain no songs.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original native Game Boy top-down pirate action game with anchor melee, cannon shooting, enemies, collisions, level state, pause/menu transitions and hUGE/CBT-FX audio integration. main.c and hero.c contain concrete hardware-facing game logic.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Game Boy Color falling-piece pet-building puzzle game with multiple game types, special pieces, hinting, variable difficulty, music, statistics and two-player link-cable play. gameplay.c implements drop progression, linked-player game-over handling and input/state updates.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Mega Drive music-cartridge software, with track browser, ordered/shuffle playback, on-screen visualizations and custom cartridge LED-array support. Main C program uses native VDP/joypad routines; XGM driver manages the Z80 sound processor.
- https://github.com/andwn/ym2017/blob/2bcf5dce5a338ef390748b5e426c95a3ae02d6d3/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Concrete interactive music application/ROM, not only an album dump or music files. Custom LED visualization is optional hardware functionality; emulator/flashcart operation is author-reported. GPLv3 repository licence; SGDK-derived XGM/LZ4W/font components and credited music/art remain component-specific. Music rights must not be inferred from engine licensing alone.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native NES homebrew recreation of Undertale’s Lesser Dog battle. Assembly main source initializes PPU/APU/RAM and includes custom graphics, text, input, coroutine/generator and battle/menu modules. It creates a real NES ROM rather than wrapping the PC game in a CPU interpreter.
- https://github.com/nukep/undertale-nes/blob/81b663a1c72984071df4d1a70e722ade651b928c/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Explicit WIP single-battle demo, not full Undertale port. Substantial NES initialization/render/text/menu source; no completeness or playability claim independently verified. No project licence file or README grant found in inspected tree; original Undertale characters/art remain separate third-party rights. Bundled asm6 tooling is not a licence for the game recreation.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original computer-themed NES RPG made for Game Off 2021. C main controls desktop/window-style UI and game state; dungeon source creates sector layouts, tracks completion and loads entities, supported by MMC3 mapper and native assembly runtime.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native GBA Lua game framework built from parts of the author’s BlindJump C++ engine. Concrete BPCoreEngine.cpp embeds Lua APIs and allocator; gba_platform.cpp accesses GBA hardware. Lua manifest/build script packages resources/scripts behind a native engine ROM to enable small games.
- https://github.com/evanbowman/BPCore-Engine/blob/87d734ad2ba99b607b48dcf442197dedcba8436a/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

Explicitly limited to relatively small minigames due to Lua overhead on GBA. Native engine plus Lua interpreter does not constitute game-specific predecessor CPU emulation. ### Identity (reviewed)

Reusable C++ toolkit for analyzing 8086 DOS MZ executables and comparing instruction-level reconstructions even when routine and data layout changes. Includes header, map, signature, pointer and duplicate-code tools plus an instruction decoder and analysis library.
- https://github.com/neuviemeporte/mzretools/blob/5ee06382d15364a6161d8d963e0b1ae317ffa0c1/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Original Qualitas 386SWAT protected-mode debugger source archive with extensive assembly, instruction decoding, source-symbol tools, VCPI/DPMI integration and Windows VxD components. Supports debugging native protected-mode and virtual-8086 clients.

### Classification (reviewed)

SWAT.ASM defines mixed use16/use32 segments and core state; SWAT_DBG.ASM implements debugger services. Substantial original implementation rather than binary-only package. Archive includes alternate historical directories, not separate projects. No explicit project-wide licence grant located in tree; inspected source and manual retain Qualitas all-rights-reserved notices. Public source availability is not an unrestricted reuse grant.

Reusable Python tools connecting GDB/pygdb to DOSBox guest registers and memory, processing instruction traces, decoding selected DOS system calls and substituting multi-instruction patterns with readable pseudo-operations.
- https://github.com/johndoe31415/dosdebug/blob/51c6fe4a7797f1030ea4c8c46d2fdd3518f72c73/dos_strace.py
- https://github.com/johndoe31415/dosdebug/blob/51c6fe4a7797f1030ea4c8c46d2fdd3518f72c73/LICENSE

- https://github.com/johndoe31415/dosdebug/commit/51c6fe4a7797f1030ea4c8c46d2fdd3518f72c73

### Classification (reviewed)

DosBox.py defines guest register/memory/backtrace GDB commands; dos_strace.py dispatches DOS call interpretation. System-call coverage is intentionally selective and backtrace unwinding described as unreliable. GPL-3.0 text and README declaration.

### Identity (reviewed)

Small Python implementation for locating early Microsoft CodeView NB00 data appended to DOS MZ executables and reading module/public-symbol subsections. Intended for symbols produced by Microsoft LINK 3.x through 5.03.
- https://github.com/philpem/CodeViewParser/blob/22f76ad872ee9ad25737b2ba373088e533216579/symbolik.py
- https://github.com/philpem/CodeViewParser/commit/22f76ad872ee9ad25737b2ba373088e533216579
- https://github.com/philpem/CodeViewParser/tree/22f76ad872ee9ad25737b2ba373088e533216579

### Classification (reviewed)

symbolik.py searches the EXE tail for NB0 signatures, decodes subsection directory and module/public records. Prototype limitations include hard-coded annexA.exe input and PublicSymbol assigning self.segment from undefined seg rather than its segment parameter. No execution to verify or repair. No licence file or explicit permission grant found in single-file tree; reuse rights unclear.

### Identity (reviewed)

Preserved OS2Linux source library implementing OS/2 C APIs on Linux, allowing applications with available source to be recompiled and linked natively. Contains file, process, memory, module, semaphore, pipe and resource API modules.
- https://github.com/OS2World/LINUX-SYSTEM-OS2Linux/blob/f30ce1653b09b42128bc532e99cde48a44278316/README

### Classification (reviewed)

os2/filemgr.c implements DosOpen by translating paths, flags, file handles and errno to OS/2 semantics. Test programs included. Source-compatible API library, not an OS/2 executable emulator; overall API completeness unverified. Common Public License 1.0 in COPYING and source headers; some headers also carry additional redistribution wording.

### Identity (reviewed)

Preserved Ivan Skytte Jørgensen OS2API library mapping OS/2 DOSCALL APIs onto Win32. Recompiled OS/2 program source links against OS2API.LIB to execute natively under Windows NT.
- https://github.com/OS2World/WINNT-OS2API/blob/7bef3401fd3044df5fffb91fff2b55991f1c232d/license.txt
- https://github.com/OS2World/WINNT-OS2API/blob/7bef3401fd3044df5fffb91fff2b55991f1c232d/filemgr0.cpp

- https://github.com/OS2World/WINNT-OS2API/commit/7bef3401fd3044df5fffb91fff2b55991f1c232d

### Classification (reviewed)

filemgr0.cpp maps timestamps/attributes/error codes between NT and OS/2; substantial process, memory, pipe and queue modules present. README claims over 110 DOSCALL functions; coverage not independently tested. zlib-style permissive licence: origin attribution and altered-version marking required. README informal BSD-alike wording does not replace actual licence.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original 1995 assembly unpacker for programs that started as DOS COM images and were packed into EXE form. Uses Intel 386 debug registers and real-mode execution to recover the unpacked image.
- https://github.com/maximmasiutin/comunp/blob/d2f65e5551d8dde4c6afcc973e910c7edf2a9030/license.txt
- https://github.com/maximmasiutin/comunp/blob/d2f65e5551d8dde4c6afcc973e910c7edf2a9030/comunp.asm

- https://github.com/maximmasiutin/comunp/commit/d2f65e5551d8dde4c6afcc973e910c7edf2a9030

### Classification (reviewed)

comunp.asm checks 386 availability, loads fixed RABBIT.EXE input, programs debug-register breakpoint state and handles unpacking. General unpacker, not a specific-game emulation wrapper. GPL-2.0 licence text committed; original author header remains.

### Identity (reviewed)

Reconstruction of Heinz Hombergs DOS-Expander 4.0 file manager and program menu, with Borland Pascal/assembly sources plus a modern-terminal Rust behavioral port. Rebuild scripts compare recovered modules against original binaries.
- https://github.com/retro-ware/dx4-re/blob/7198e74c6b9cd317137a7898e032111292435e20/rust/PORTING.md

Browser.PAS reconstructs directory enumeration and menu UI. README claims matching DX.EXE/DX.COM/DXM.EXE, but DXMAIN.EXE has placeholder units and no full link; no independent byte-match validation. ### Identity (reviewed)

C/assembly reconstruction of Chris Van Genderen pcZIP V1.00.000B/D parallel-port Iomega ZIP/Jaz driver and manager binaries. Includes byte-identical assembly baseline, C command dispatch and differential hardware-protocol oracle.
- https://github.com/onionmixer/pcZIP-remade/blob/efec0814858ffbbf01638c8c9327c044087a231f/03_src/stage2/mgr/pzfdmain.c
- https://github.com/onionmixer/pcZIP-remade/blob/efec0814858ffbbf01638c8c9327c044087a231f/03_src/stage2/pzdisr.c

### Classification (reviewed)

pzdisr.c implements block-request parsing, INIT/READ/WRITE/media/BPB/IOCTL dispatch; pzfdmain.c reconstructs shared non-destructive manager paths. Claims functional-equivalent full driver and matching manager paths are unverified. Destructive partition/format/MBR and interactive manager UI explicitly excluded. GPL-3.0-or-later grant notice. LICENSE explicitly says full GPL text is absent and must accompany redistribution; binary baseline licence is attributed to original author by repository.

calcfrac.c contains substantive fractal worklist, tesseral, symmetry and render-engine code; bcfract.mak includes numerous C and optimized ASM modules. ### Identity (reviewed)

Historical ELF system files recovered from SAIL, with PDP-11 operating-system/network source, linker code, manuals and PDP-10 host-side tools. The archive explicitly records conversion of SAIL 36-bit words into ASCII octets.

### Identity (reviewed)

Preserved 1990s Pilot VT100 application source and later Palm OS 4 SDK compatibility patch, implementing serial terminal I/O, escape parser, screen handling and configurable keys.
- https://github.com/unwiredben/vt100-for-palm-os/blob/f953dae5be61e58e0aeb928de18bef3ed8fda9e9/term.c

### Classification (reviewed)

Reviewed serial settings/screen state and VT100 parsing routines. Latest commit records Palm IIIxe/PalmOS 3.5.2 usage and specific known receive-overrun/keymap bug. docs/LICENSE is a 3-clause BSD-style grant; term.c retains older non-commercial-only header, so mixed historical notices should be checked before reuse.

### Identity (reviewed)

Mats Nordstrom’s early-1980s FORTRAN Interlisp interpreter, preserved in original form and translated with f2c into a subsequently hand-maintained portable C interpreter, with Lisp libraries, image-building scripts, manuals and regression tests.
- https://github.com/blakemcbride/LISPF4/blob/276f5c470febd61404c133296910041ad6f7092b/README.md

### Identity (reviewed)

Independent TypeScript Centurion minicomputer emulator with substantial CPU6 opcode/addressing-mode implementation, RAM/ROM/diagnostic/multiplexer cards and debugging support.

## Research event notes

Bounded broad discovery with programmatic URL screening before pinned implementation/build/licence inspection and title/lineage deduplication. Not an exhaustive inventory of every platform. Source completeness, rights and AI attribution remain qualified. No upstream software executed; all earlier catalogue records preserved.
